- **squadai-manager**: SquadAI configuration manager. Use this agent when you need to manage the project's AI agent configuration, run governance checks, apply policy changes, or troubleshoot agent setup issues. This agent has access to all SquadAI operations via the MCP server. ([PedroMosquera/squadai](https://github.com/PedroMosquera/squadai/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/squadai-manager.md) · ★10 · 2 copies)
- **squadra-specialist-agent**: Use: - tlc-spec-driven - squadra-ui - squadra-design - squadra-architecture - squadra-security - squadra-testing - squadra-api-contract - squadra-database - squadra-observability ([lidiagaldino/squadra](https://github.com/lidiagaldino/squadra/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/squadra-specialist-agent.md))
- **squads-team**: Invoke for work spanning roles or independent workstreams. Coordinate ownership, dependencies, workers, QA, review and integration. Single-role work selects its owner; bugs go to squad-fix; unframed ideas go to squad-product. ([danh121097/squad-skills](https://github.com/danh121097/squad-skills/blob/HEAD/agents/squads-team.md))
- **squall**: Use for an adversarial bug, crash and defect review of a diff before a PR is ready, or to triage a bug report. Read-only; ranked findings with trigger and minimal fix. ([saimskywalker/lumenparty](https://github.com/saimskywalker/lumenparty/blob/HEAD/agents/squall.md))
- **square-expert**: Use this agent when implementing or debugging Square API integrations — payments, orders, catalog, terminal, loyalty, gift cards, bookings, OAuth setup, or webhook verification. Also use when troubleshooting Square SDK errors or debugging API responses. ([Spearlance/spearlance-os](https://github.com/Spearlance/spearlance-os/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/square-expert.md))
- **square-meter-pricing-engine-fellow** ([suphiozdedee/antigravity-skills](https://github.com/suphiozdedee/antigravity-skills/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/square-meter-pricing-engine-fellow.md))
- **squares**: A squares league holds boards (`square_boards`), each a 10 by 10 grid on one NFL game. ([zimm11/football-weekly-picks](https://github.com/zimm11/football-weekly-picks/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/squares.md))
- **squarespace**: Reference for agents working with the Squarespace API layer. All methods are on `ContentSaveClient` in `src/services/content-save.ts`. Types in `src/services/content-save-types.ts`. ([timncox/squarespace-mcp](https://github.com/timncox/squarespace-mcp/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/squarespace.md) · ★9)
- **squash_agent**: Git history cleaner assistant. Analyzes the recent local commits on the current branch and safely squashes them into cohesive, logical commits following Conventional Commits and the ConcepTracker layers. Uses 'git reset --soft' to rewrite history without losing data. ([Presmanes3/ConcepTracker](https://github.com/Presmanes3/ConcepTracker/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/squash_agent.agent.md))
- **squint**: QA and reviewer: checks each task, edge cases, test report. ([mlemxy/seal-team](https://github.com/mlemxy/seal-team/blob/HEAD/agents/squint.md))
- **SQWR Accessibility Audit**: Run the SQWR Accessibility audit — checks 20 criteria across 4 verification levels ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-ACCESSIBILITY-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR AI Governance Audit**: Run the SQWR AI Governance audit — checks 17 criteria across 4 verification levels ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-AI-GOVERNANCE-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Brainstorm**: Pre-implementation analysis agent — evaluates scope, reversibility, and approach plurality before any implementation starts. Returns Go/Clarify/Scope-Reduce verdict with structured plan. ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-BRAINSTORM.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Code Quality Audit**: Run the SQWR Code Quality audit — checks 15 criteria across 4 verification levels ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-CODE-QUALITY-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Deployment Gate**: Run the SQWR Deployment pre-production gate — all blocking checks must pass before merging to main ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-DEPLOYMENT-GATE.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Design Audit**: Run the SQWR Design audit — checks 18 criteria across 4 verification levels ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-DESIGN-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Full Audit**: Run the full SQWR audit suite — 8 weighted domains + 1 optional (Resilience), global score per AUDIT-INDEX.md ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-FULL-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Observability Audit**: Run the SQWR Observability audit — checks 18 criteria across 4 verification levels ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-OBSERVABILITY-AUDIT.md) · ★2)

- **SQWR Resilience Audit**: Run the SQWR Resilience audit — checks circuit breakers, retry patterns, graceful degradation, health checks, timeouts, and dependency isolation ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-RESILIENCE-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Risk Score**: Calculate composite risk score across all audit domains with weighted formula — produces a single 0-100 score representing overall project health ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-RISK-SCORE.md) · ★2)
- **SQWR Security Audit**: Run the SQWR Security audit — checks 14 criteria across 4 verification levels ([samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit](https://github.com/samsam007b/claude-code-standards-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/AGENT-SECURITY-AUDIT.md) · ★2)
- **sr-a1-anchor**: stock-research 模式 1/2 的 A1 数据锚采证路。仅由 stock-research 控制者派发，勿直接调用。 ([kavenGw/GSStock](https://github.com/kavenGw/GSStock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sr-a1-anchor.md) · ★3)
- **sr-a2-thesis**: stock-research 模式 1/2 的 A2 论点验证采证路。仅由 stock-research 控制者派发，勿直接调用。 ([kavenGw/GSStock](https://github.com/kavenGw/GSStock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sr-a2-thesis.md) · ★3)
- **sr-a3-lens**: stock-research 模式 1/2 的 A3 lens 专项采证路。仅由 stock-research 控制者派发，勿直接调用。 ([kavenGw/GSStock](https://github.com/kavenGw/GSStock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sr-a3-lens.md) · ★3)
- **sr-arbiter**: SuperReview 异构多模型协同 Review 的 L4 仲裁层。读取 aggregator.py 输出的 aggregated.json，对 critical 级别 + needs_arbitration 列表做逐条复核，结合代码上下文做业务校准，输出最终 verdict (approve/request_changes/needs_human) 和 prioritized findings list。**强调严谨判断而非简单汇总。** ([heresun/SuperReview](https://github.com/heresun/SuperReview/blob/HEAD/agents/sr-arbiter.md))
- **sr-backend**: Principal Backend Engineer (AI-SR-FULL) — implementa backend NestJS + PostgreSQL multi-tenant + BullMQ dentro del Modulith y publica el contrato de API tipado. Usar para endpoints, servicios, entidades, migraciones, jobs o contratos de API. No implementa frontend ni decide UX/producto. ([SleyiW/iWana-neXt](https://github.com/SleyiW/iWana-neXt/blob/HEAD/.opencode/agents/sr-backend.md) · 2 copies)
- **sr-bugs**: Reviews staged git diff for bugs, logic errors, null safety issues, edge cases, and error handling problems. Launch this agent when performing staged code review. ([ben3329/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ben3329/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/staged-review/agents/sr-bugs.md))
- **sr-change-navigator**: Use this agent FIRST in a PR review, before any other reviewer. It reads the diff and PR description and produces a "reading guide" — what the change really does (intent vs. incidental churn), the business domain it touches, the entry points, the optimal order to read the files, and the risk hotspots. - **srk-verifier**: Adversarial Shrinkage verifier — tries to prove a completed reduction BROKE behavior, by re-deriving evidence independently and checking the transform's known failure modes. - **srr-investigation**: The investigation and post-incident seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer when the work starts from an event (an error in hand, or an incident nothing caught). For an error in hand it first says how bad, what to do now and whom to engage. It places the event in the chain, investigates it in the logs, monitoring and code the room can reach, and says where the finding belongs (a measure, an alert, a runbook branch, or the room's map of the system), for the other seats to build. Read-only; not for fixing the code or running the incident. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-investigation.md))
- **srr-observability**: The observability seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to understand a capability's journeys and the code behind each, find who depends on it, decide what "healthy" means per journey, and build the health check and its views. Files what cannot yet be seen as instrumentation issues for the developers. Not for alerting or runbooks; those are other seats. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-observability.md))
- **srr-review**: The review seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to check one output from one discipline, cold, having made none of it, or to check the chief's proposal of the five, placement or report. It reports findings and never edits the output. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-review.md))
- **srr-runbooks**: The runbooks and incident response seat of the service reliability room. Launched by the room's chief engineer to write and assemble a capability's home (alert index, health check page, component pages, runbooks in the runbook model's form) and to run the tabletop walk that tests it. Not for choosing measures or alerts; it takes those from the other seats. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-runbooks.md))
- **srr-steward**: The steward of the service reliability room, its practice owner. Launched by the room's chief engineer after each report, with what the run recorded, or when the person says the room got something wrong, or directly by the person ("update the room", "add a seat"). It gathers the person's reactions, the misses and second-pass findings, proposes a change to the room's skill or seats when they add up to one, and, once the person approves and an independent review passes, applies it to this copy of the room and logs it in CHANGES.md. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-steward.md))
- **srr-transition**: The service transition seat of the service reliability room, held from the side of the team that runs production. Launched first by the room's chief engineer to find what either team has already recorded and what is live with too little behind it, to turn what only the development team knows into specific requests with proposals attached, and to flag steps an operator could not follow. Not a gate and not a handover checklist; it drafts nothing the other seats own. ([ochocinco0601/honey-toast](https://github.com/ochocinco0601/honey-toast/blob/HEAD/agents/srr-transition.md))
- **SRS Crosscheck Agent**: Cross-check SRS generation outputs for traceability, normative style, and section-structure compliance. ([iannuzza/Deterministic_Reqs_workflow](https://github.com/iannuzza/Deterministic_Reqs_workflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/srs-crosscheck.agent.md))
- **SRS Gen Spec Agent**: Generate a project-specific SRS document package from Stage 1 and Stage 2 micro-architecture artifacts with full requirement traceability. ([iannuzza/Deterministic_Reqs_workflow](https://github.com/iannuzza/Deterministic_Reqs_workflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/srs_gen_spec.agent.md))
- **SRS LaTeX Crosscheck Agent**: Cross-check SRS markdown-to-LaTeX output for LaTeX validity, source inclusion, and formatting consistency. ([iannuzza/Deterministic_Reqs_workflow](https://github.com/iannuzza/Deterministic_Reqs_workflow/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/srs-latex-crosscheck.agent.md))

([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-idempotency-attacker.md))
- **sstack-malformed-attacker**: Malformed lens attacker. Attacks every mapped surface for wrong types, corrupt structures, format violations, delimiter hazards, and unvalidated parsing. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-malformed-attacker.md))
- **sstack-missing-attacker**: Missing lens attacker. Attacks every mapped surface for absent fields, null/None/undefined, falsy traps, empty inputs, and boundary leaks. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-missing-attacker.md))
- **sstack-ordering-attacker**: Ordering lens attacker. Attacks every mapped surface for out-of-order execution, step-skipping in multi-stage workflows, inverted pipeline calls, and premature finalization. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-ordering-attacker.md))
- **sstack-ownership-attacker**: Ownership lens attacker. Attacks authorization decisions across function, data, and field level: BOLA/IDOR, BOPLA, deny-by-default, least privilege, permission-not-role checks, token/session integrity, header/IP bypasses, CORS/CSRF, cross-tenant, and intermediary delegation. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-ownership-attacker.md))
- **sstack-research**: Analyst agent for codebase and domain research ([ormastes/simple](https://github.com/ormastes/simple/blob/HEAD/tools/claude-plugin/sstack-research/agents/research.md) · ★2)
- **sstack-resource-exhaustion-attacker**: Resource-exhaustion lens attacker. Attacks every mapped surface for connection pool exhaustion, rate limits, memory ceilings, payload limits, and disk pressure. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-resource-exhaustion-attacker.md))
- **sstack-security-attacker**: Security lens attacker. Attacks every mapped surface for SQL injection, OS command injection, path traversal, JWT token tampering, and insecure deserialization. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-security-attacker.md))
- **sstack-spec**: QA Lead agent for BDD/TDD spec writing (test-first) ([ormastes/simple](https://github.com/ormastes/simple/blob/HEAD/tools/claude-plugin/sstack-spec/agents/spec.md) · ★2)
- **sstack-state-attacker**: State lens attacker. Attacks every mapped surface for stale cached reads, write-through to caller data, partial updates after failure, escaped internal references, and invalid lifecycle transitions. Invoked as a subagent after the orchestrator writes the surface map. Rubric and Report format arrive inline in the dispatch. ([mhenke/sstack](https://github.com/mhenke/sstack/blob/HEAD/agents/sstack-state-attacker.md))
- **sstate-optimizer**: Use when diagnosing sstate-cache misses, analyzing task hash changes, comparing sigdata with bitbake-diffsigs, tuning SSTATE_DIR/SSTATE_MIRRORS, optimizing shared state across machines, debugging why a recipe rebuilds unexpectedly, or understanding BB_HASHCHECK_FUNCTION, task signatures, and setscene tasks. - **stability-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer that checks feature work against its stability level (Prototype/Alpha/Beta/GA), flagging both missing requirements and premature engineering. Use to review a feature, a diff, or a promotion against a stability level, or, after fixes, to verify that an earlier review's findings are resolved. ([exalynt/forge](https://github.com/exalynt/forge/blob/HEAD/claude/agents/stability-reviewer.md))
- **stability-reviewer**: KIOST 정총무1.0 (국내여비 실비정산) Next.js 앱의 안정성(stability)을 전문적으로 검토하는 에이전트. "안정성 검토", "stability review", "이 앱 안 터지는지 봐줘", "배포 전에 점검해줘" 같은 요청에 사용한다. ([shootto8-prog/KIOST_TRIP_](https://github.com/shootto8-prog/KIOST_TRIP_/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stability-reviewer.md))
- **stability-tester**: Tests whether an app stays healthy over time and under repetition - memory and DOM-node leaks across repeated navigation, console errors and failed requests during long sessions, behaviour under slow or failing network, resource exhaustion under sustained load, and flaky tests (repeated runs, random order) - and reports each instability with how to reproduce it. Runs only against local or disposable instances. ([eddremonts86/ai-os](https://github.com/eddremonts86/ai-os/blob/HEAD/ai-config/agents/stability-tester.md) · ★1)
- **stabilization-assessor**: Read-only stabilization assessor for polyrepos spanning AngularJS 1.x, Angular 17 (NX 17.3.x), and React 19 front ends (with Webpack 5 Module Federation MFEs), Java 21 / Spring Boot 2.7.18 and 3.5 / Hibernate / Maven 3.8+ services, Oracle 19c and AlloyDB (PostgreSQL) data, SOLR 9.x search, and Redis 7.2 cache. Maps topology, traces critical runtime paths, and produces evidence-linked Candidate findings with confidence states. Never edits code and never performs security review. Delegate discovery and finding-generation here to keep audit work isolated from any implementation context. ([loganmcleod/legacy-stabilizer](https://github.com/loganmcleod/legacy-stabilizer/blob/HEAD/agents/stabilization-assessor.md))
- **stabilization-manager**: Orchestrate the stabilization phase by remediating confirmed bugs, performance issues, and security findings from qa-manager artifacts, then prepare the slice for QA re-validation. ([eolarte/cqrs-lite](https://github.com/eolarte/cqrs-lite/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/stabilization-manager.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **stabilize**: Stabilization phase agent — fix broken deployments or rollback. ([Bitmia-ai/RedEye](https://github.com/Bitmia-ai/RedEye/blob/HEAD/agents/stabilize.md) · ★6)
- **stabilize**: Spawned by implement-plan-module, Stage 1. Not for direct use — it needs step context only that orchestrator has. Stabilization step agent: applies the stabilization item ids it receives — contract artifacts, database changes, interface and signature sync, stubs, configuration, shared test infrastructure. Writes no behaviour and no test. Stack-agnostic; every command, file location and disable mechanism comes from the module conventions the orchestrator points it at. ([ds-usova/tdd-sdlc](https://github.com/ds-usova/tdd-sdlc/blob/HEAD/plugin/agents/stabilize.md))
- **stabilize**: Master orchestrator for application-wide stabilization: runs a security-hardening pass, then stabilizes feature modules one by one (delegating to the stabilize-module agent), then a final verification sweep. Use for a whole-app hardening effort rather than a single module. ([MadheshSM/learning](https://github.com/MadheshSM/learning/blob/HEAD/Angular/.claude/agents/stabilize.md))
- **stabilize-module**: Full stabilization pass on a single Angular feature module: types all `any`, adds error handling, fixes subscription teardown, migrates to Angular 19 signal APIs, extracts interfaces/enums/constants, audits naming/spelling, SCSS theme compliance, i18n readiness, removes dead code, simplifies, and writes tests — with a verify-after-every-phase discipline. ([MadheshSM/learning](https://github.com/MadheshSM/learning/blob/HEAD/Angular/.claude/agents/stabilize-module.md))
- **stabilizer**: 팩토리 안정화·회귀 방지 게이트 전문가. skills/myharness 본문·references 변경(중대 blast-radius — 모든 생성 하네스에 전파)에 대해 정책 감사(run-policy-audit.sh)·외부 리뷰 게이트(external-review-loop)·회귀 드라이런을 조율한다. "고도화", "안정화", "회귀 방지", "스킬 개선 배포 전 검증", "정책 감사" 등 팩토리 정본을 고치고 안전하게 굳히는 작업에 사용. ([cookyman74/my_harness](https://github.com/cookyman74/my_harness/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stabilizer.md) · ★89)
- **stabilizer**: Fixes a red main by root cause; never weakens a test or grows a baseline. ([LgbtBsod/AI-EVOLVE](https://github.com/LgbtBsod/AI-EVOLVE/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/stabilizer.md) · ★2)
- **stabilizer**: Runs the new or changed Playwright specs, diagnoses failures with heal-analyze and the Playwright MCP server, fixes what is the test's fault, hardens locators, and reports. Use as the last step after script-reviewer, or any time a suite needs to be re-stabilized. ([jkhushboo/playwright-ai-scaffold](https://github.com/jkhushboo/playwright-ai-scaffold/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/stabilizer.agent.md) · 2 copies)
- **stable-abi-checker**: Use this agent to audit Python stable ABI and limited API compliance in C extension code.\n\n&lt;example&gt;\nUser: Check if my C extension is compatible with the stable ABI.\nAgent: I will determine whether the extension claims limited API compliance, verify that claim against the stable ABI surface, check for private API usage and direct struct access, and assess migration feasibility if not currently using the limited API.\n&lt;/example&gt; ([ReviewToolkits/cext-review-toolkit](https://github.com/ReviewToolkits/cext-review-toolkit/blob/HEAD/plugins/cext-review-toolkit/agents/stable-abi-checker.md) · ★29)

- **stablyai-orca**: Orca는 여러 CLI 코딩 에이전트를 각자 독립된 git worktree에서 동시에 실행하고 한 곳에서 추적하는 데스크톱 애플리케이션이다. stablyai가 MIT 라이선스로 공개했고, README는 스스로를 "The AI Orchestrator for 100x builders"로 소개하며 Codex와 Claude Code와 OpenCode와 Pi를 나란히 실행하는 화면을 첫 이미지로 내세운다.
